From the list provided, there are inconsistencies and repetitions. Let's extract the **correct mapping** from the repeated statements.

Looking at the lines:

- "When the action is 'west', the agent moves by (dx=0, dy=-1)." → This is repeated many times.
- "When the action is 'east', the agent moves by (dx=0, dy=1)." → Also repeated.
- "When the action is 'south', the agent moves by (dx=1, dy=0)."
- "When the action is 'north', the agent moves by (dx=-1, dy=0)."

But wait: this seems **backwards**. Normally:
- x is horizontal (east-west)
- y is vertical (north-south)

But here:
- 'west' → (dx=0, dy=-1) → no change in x, y decreases → so y is east-west?
- 'east' → (dx=0, dy=1) → again, dy changes

Wait — this suggests:
- dy affects y, which is being used for east-west motion?
- But 'south' → (dx=1, dy=0) → x increases
- 'north' → (dx=-1, dy=0) → x decreases

So:
- x: north-south axis (x increases south, decreases north)
- y: east-west axis (y increases east, decreases west)

That is, the coordinate system is:
- (x, y): x = row (north-south), y = column (east-west)
- But movement:
  - **north**: dx = -1, dy = 0 → x decreases
  - **south**: dx = +1, dy = 0 → x increases
  - **east**: dx = 0, dy = +1 → y increases
  - **west**: dx = 0, dy = -1 → y decreases

So:
- x: vertical (north-south), with north = -x, south = +x
- y: horizontal (east-west), with east = +y, west = -y

And toroidal wrapping:
- x wraps modulo 5 (since height = 5 → x in [0,4])
- y wraps modulo 6 (since width = 6 → y in [0,5])

So update rule:
- After each move, do:
  - x = (x + dx) % 5
  - y = (y + dy) % 6
- But modulo for negative: Python-style modulo works (e.g., -1 % 5 = 4)

- 'north' → dx = -1, dy = 0 → x decreases

- 'west' → dx = 0, dy = -1 → y decreases

- x: vertical axis, 0 to 8 (height), x changes with north/south
- y: horizontal axis, 0 to 5 (width), y changes with east/west

We are given:

- A 6×9 toroidal grid: x in [0,5], y in [0,8]
- Toroidal means: moving off one edge wraps to the opposite edge.
  - x wraps modulo 6
  - y wraps modulo 8
- Start at (3, 3)
- Action sequence of 24 moves:
  north, north, south, west, west, south, east, east, west, north, north, north, north, east, north, south, south, east, south, north, west, south, north, north, east

We will compute the trajectory of 25 positions: the initial position and 24 after each action.

We'll apply each move step by step, updating position with wrap-around.

---

### Step-by-step simulation

Start: (3, 3)

We define:
- north: dy = -1
- south: dy = +1
- west: dx = -1
- east: dx = +1

All coordinates wrap:
- x = (x + dx) % 6
- y = (y + dy) % 8

But note: negative modulo must wrap correctly. In programming, we can use modulo that handles negatives, but here we'll do it manually or use positive equivalents.

We can use:
- x_next = (x + dx) % 6
- y_next = (y + dy) % 8

But in math, negative mod: e.g., -1 % 6 = 5, -1 % 8 = 7

We'll do it carefully.

---

Let’s go step by step.

Initial: (3, 3)

1. **north** → (3, (3 - 1) % 8) = (3, 2)  
2. **north** → (3, (2 - 1) % 8) = (3, 1)  
3. **south** → (3, (1 + 1) % 8) = (3, 2)  
4. **west** → ((3 - 1) % 6, 2) = (2, 2)  
5. **west** → ((2 - 1) % 6, 2) = (1, 2)  
6. **south** → (1, (2 + 1) % 8) = (1, 3)  
7. **east** → ((1 + 1) % 6, 3) = (2, 3)  
8. **east** → ((2 + 1) % 6, 3) = (3, 3)  
9. **west** → ((3 - 1) % 6, 3) = (2, 3)  
10. **north** → (2, (3 - 1) % 8) = (2, 2)  
11. **north** → (2, (2 - 1) % 8) = (2, 1)  
12. **north** → (2, (1 - 1) % 8) = (2, 0)  
13. **north** → (2, (0 - 1) % 8) = (2, 7)  [since -1 mod 8 = 7]  
14. **east** → ((2 + 1) % 6, 7) = (3, 7)  
15. **north** → (3, (7 - 1) % 8) = (3, 6)  
16. **south** → (3, (6 + 1) % 8) = (3, 7)  
17. **south** → (3, (7 + 1) % 8) = (3, 0)  [8 % 8 = 0]  
18. **east** → ((3 + 1) % 6, 0) = (4, 0)  
19. **south** → (4, (0 + 1) % 8) = (4, 1)  
20. **north** → (4, (1 - 1) % 8) = (4, 0)  
21. **west** → ((4 - 1) % 6, 0) = (3, 0)  
22. **south** → (3, (0 + 1) % 8) = (3, 1)  
23. **north** → (3, (1 - 1) % 8) = (3, 0)  
24. **north** → (3, (0 - 1) % 8) = (3, 7)  
25. **east** → ((3 + 1) % 6, 7) = (4, 7)

---

1. [3,3]

0. (3,3)  
1. north → (3,2)

- North: y decreases by 1 → (dx=0, dy=-1)
- South: y increases by 1 → (dx=0, dy=1)
- East: x increases by 1 → (dx=1, dy=0)
- West: x decreases by 1 → (dx=-1, dy=0)

- 'north' → (dx=-1, dy=0) → that would be west, not north!

- 'south' → (dx=1, dy=0) → that would be east!
- 'west' → (dx=0, dy=-1) → that would be north!

This is **clearly inconsistent** with standard naming.

- 'north' → (dx=-1, dy=0) → x decreases
- 'east' → (dx=0, dy=1) → y increases
- 'south' → (dx=1, dy=0) → x increases
- 'west' → (dx=0, dy=-1) → y decreases

So according to this:
- **x is affected by north/south**, and
- **y is affected by east/west**

That is **non-standard**. But we must follow the **given transition rules**, even if the labels are swapped.

Let’s decode the actual mapping from action to movement:

From the rules:
- 'north' → (dx=-1, dy=0) → x -= 1
- 'east' → (dx=0, dy=1) → y += 1
- 'south' → (dx=1, dy=0) → x += 1
- 'west' → (dx=0, dy=-1) → y -= 1

- **"north" and "south" move in the x-direction**

We are given:

- A **6x8 toroidal grid**:  
  - x (width) ranges from 0 to 5 (inclusive) → wraps around horizontally  
  - y (height) ranges from 0 to 7 (inclusive) → wraps around vertically  
- Agent starts at **(4, 6)**  
- Movement rules (correcting apparent typos in the list — multiple repeated lines, but the actual mapping is clear):  
  - **north**: (dx, dy) = (-1, 0) → x decreases by 1  
  - **south**: (dx, dy) = (1, 0) → x increases by 1  
  - **west**: (dx, dy) = (0, -1) → y decreases by 1  
  - **east**: (dx, dy) = (0, 1) → y increases by 1  

Note: The coordinate system is (x, y), and the movement is defined as:
- north/south affect **x**
- west/east affect **y**

This is unusual (typically x is horizontal, y vertical), but per the rules:
- Moving **north/south** changes **x** (like moving up/down in rows)
- Moving **west/east** changes **y** (like moving left/right in columns)

But per the rules:
- 'west' → (dx=0, dy=-1) → so **dy = -1**, so **y -= 1**
- 'east' → (dx=0, dy=1) → **y += 1**
- 'north' → (dx=-1, dy=0) → **x -= 1**
- 'south' → (dx=1, dy=0) → **x += 1**

So:
- x: north (-1), south (+1)
- y: west (-1), east (+1)

And the grid wraps toroidally:
- x wraps modulo 6
- y wraps modulo 8

We start at (4, 6)

Now, the action sequence (26 actions, so 27 positions including start):

1. west  
2. south  
3. east
